gSegment1Gfx = ALIGN(main_BSS_END, 16); /* segment 1's buffer, right after main's .bss, aligned for its DMA (0x80095AF0 as built; InitGraphics loads segment1_gfx there) */
/* The boot entry (asm/entry.s) clears main's .bss from gBootStack, where main_tail's copy ends, 8 bytes a step until
   the count reaches zero: the size is a multiple of 8 (0x24DD0 as built). The upper half is a symbol of its own
   because the entry builds the size with lui and ori. */
main_bss_clear_size = ALIGN(main_BSS_END, 8) - gBootStack;
main_bss_clear_size_hi = main_bss_clear_size >> 16;
/* Main's RAM limit: segment 1 is loaded right after main's .bss and must end at or before the spell bank, which is
   loaded at the fixed gEffectsBlock (0x800C0000). As built it ends at 0x800995C0, so main's code, data and .bss
   together may grow by 0x26A40 bytes; nothing else is loaded in that room. */
ASSERT(gSegment1Gfx + (segment1_gfx_ROM_END - segment1_gfx_ROM_START) <= gEffectsBlock,
       "main has outgrown its RAM: segment 1 would be loaded over the spell bank (gEffectsBlock)");

/* fixed addresses map 15's NPC records name in other binary files (not relocated) */
/* the lock on interiors_common and region_0. interiors_common is loaded for maps 13-25 and region_0 for maps 0, 13
   and 15; the maps name addresses inside them as the block's label and an offset into its one binary piece, so each
   block must stay where LoadMap loads it, at its size: a build that moves or resizes one stops here */
ASSERT(interiors_common_VRAM == D_8010FE00, "interiors_common is locked: LoadMap loads it at D_8010FE00, and the interior maps name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(interiors_common_ROM_END - interiors_common_ROM_START == 0x43BF0, "interiors_common is locked: its size changed, and the interior maps name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_0_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_0 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and maps 0 and 13 hold addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_0_ROM_END - region_0_ROM_START == 0x7790, "region_0 is locked: its size changed, and maps 0 and 13 hold addresses inside it");
/* map 15's RAM ceiling: the lowest RAM above it the code loads anything to is the ending's data (gEventBlob,
   0x801C0000), which LoadEndingScene keeps resident beside the map it loads (the ending's scenes name maps 30, 6,
   17, 26, 1, 7 and 12; nothing in the code keeps map 15 out of them). By the game's data alone the bound would be
   the region block at 0x801F8000. */
ASSERT(map_15_VRAM + (map_15_ROM_END - map_15_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_15 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");

/* fixed addresses map 32's NPC records name in other binary files (not relocated) */
/* the addresses LoadMap loads the shared blocks and the outdoor map blobs to, which map.c names and the locks assert:
   splat lists each in undefined_syms_auto.txt only while nothing at it has a name of its own, and stops once a typed
   map has a record or a name there (maps 12 and 32 start at 0x8010FE00; map 12 names field_shared's first bytes) */
D_80100000 = 0x80100000;
D_8010FE00 = 0x8010FE00;
D_801E0000 = 0x801E0000;
/* the lock on field_shared, region_5 and gameplay_common. field_shared is loaded for maps 3, 5, 7 and 12, region_5
   for maps 12 and 25, gameplay_common for every map; the maps name addresses inside them as the block's label and an
   offset into its one binary piece (DrawMapBillboards' first two textures are numbers into gameplay_common), so each
   block must stay where LoadMap loads it, at its size: a build that moves or resizes one stops here */
ASSERT(field_shared_VRAM == D_801E0000, "field_shared is locked: LoadMap loads it at D_801E0000, and maps 3, 5 and 7 hold addresses inside it");
ASSERT(field_shared_ROM_END - field_shared_ROM_START == 0x10B10, "field_shared is locked: its size changed, and maps 3, 5 and 7 hold addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_5_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_5 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and map 25 holds addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_5_ROM_END - region_5_ROM_START == 0x34A0, "region_5 is locked: its size changed, and map 25 holds addresses inside it");
ASSERT(gameplay_common_VRAM == D_80100000, "gameplay_common is locked: LoadMap loads it at D_80100000, and the maps and DrawMapBillboards name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(gameplay_common_ROM_END - gameplay_common_ROM_START == 0xFE00, "gameplay_common is locked: its size changed, and the maps and DrawMapBillboards name addresses inside it");
/* maps 12's and 32's RAM ceiling: as map 15's, the ending's data (gEventBlob, 0x801C0000), which LoadEndingScene keeps
   resident beside the map it loads (the ending's scenes name maps 30, 6, 17, 26, 1, 7 and 12; nothing in the code keeps
   map 32 out of them). By the game's data alone map 12's bound would be field_shared at 0x801E0000. */
ASSERT(map_12_VRAM + (map_12_ROM_END - map_12_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_12 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_32_VRAM + (map_32_ROM_END - map_32_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_32 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");

/* the lock on the region blocks 1-4: LoadMap loads the block a map's header names (gRegionBlocks) at
   0x801F8000, and the maps that load it name addresses inside it (display lists, textures, vertices, collision):
   each block must stay where it is loaded, at its size; a build that moves or resizes one stops here */
ASSERT(region_1_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_1 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and maps 1, 2, 3, 14 and 16 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_1_ROM_END - region_1_ROM_START == 0x7A70, "region_1 is locked: its size changed, and maps 1, 2, 3, 14 and 16 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_2_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_2 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and maps 4, 5, 6, 17, 18, 19 and 20 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_2_ROM_END - region_2_ROM_START == 0x57B0, "region_2 is locked: its size changed, and maps 4, 5, 6, 17, 18, 19 and 20 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_3_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_3 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and maps 7, 8, 21 and 22 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_3_ROM_END - region_3_ROM_START == 0x7570, "region_3 is locked: its size changed, and maps 7, 8, 21 and 22 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_4_VRAM == 0x801F8000, "region_4 is locked: LoadMap loads every region block at 0x801F8000 (gRegionBlocks), and maps 9, 10, 11, 23 and 24 name addresses inside it");
ASSERT(region_4_ROM_END - region_4_ROM_START == 0x70E0, "region_4 is locked: its size changed, and maps 9, 10, 11, 23 and 24 name addresses inside it");

/* the RAM ceiling of maps 0, 1, 3, 5, 6, 7, 30, as the typed maps' before them: the ending's data (gEventBlob, 0x801C0000),
   which LoadEndingScene keeps resident beside the map it loads, is the lowest RAM above any map blob */
ASSERT(map_00_VRAM + (map_00_ROM_END - map_00_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_00 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_01_VRAM + (map_01_ROM_END - map_01_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_01 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_03_VRAM + (map_03_ROM_END - map_03_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_03 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_05_VRAM + (map_05_ROM_END - map_05_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_05 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_06_VRAM + (map_06_ROM_END - map_06_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_06 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_07_VRAM + (map_07_ROM_END - map_07_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_07 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");
ASSERT(map_30_VRAM + (map_30_ROM_END - map_30_ROM_START) <= gEventBlob, "map_30 has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the ending's data (gEventBlob), which the ending keeps resident beside the map it loads");

/* Brian's model's link address (configure.py writes it into the linker script; the original's 0x80206000, the
   address splat extracts and names its records at): lower it for a larger model, above the largest animation */
brian_model_LINK = 0x80206000;
/* Brian's RAM: his animation files at LoadBrianAnimation's buffer, an immediate in its code; each file below
   the model; the model below the overlay slot (the NPCs' held objects in play, the title, intro and ending
   overlays). The largest animation and the model share the RAM between the buffer and the slot. */
ASSERT(brian_anim_00_VRAM == 0x80200000, "brian_anim_00: LoadBrianAnimation loads it to 0x80200000, an immediate in its code (BRIAN_ANIM_BUFFER)");
ASSERT(brian_anim_00_VRAM + (brian_anim_00_ROM_END - brian_anim_00_ROM_START) <= brian_model_VRAM, "brian_anim_00 has outgrown the animation buffer: it would overwrite Brian's model");

ASSERT(brian_model_VRAM + (brian_model_ROM_END - brian_model_ROM_START) <= npc_held_objects_VRAM, "brian_model has outgrown its room: it would run into the overlay slot (the NPCs' held objects; the title, intro and credits overlays)");
/* the Controller Pak manager's frame-buffer clear loop (boot only, before InitBrian): splat pairs its lui with the step
   addiu as one value; fixed, so it is no record of Brian's animation files (symbol_addrs.txt) */
D_80200010 = 0x80200010;

/* the save system's record read (src/game/save_menu.c, include/game/save_format.h): ScanSaveList reads a note's whole 0x80-byte record into
   gSaveRecordBuffer, which gSaveRecordBufferTail follows (the two sized to SAVE_RECORD_BYTES): no other variable may lie in
   those 0x80 bytes, or a load would overwrite it */
ASSERT(gSaveRecordBufferTail == gSaveRecordBuffer + 0x68,
       "the save record buffer's tail has moved: a 0x80-byte record read fills gSaveRecordBuffer and the 0x18 bytes after it");
ASSERT(gPakReadBuffer >= gSaveRecordBuffer + 0x80,
       "the save record buffer has lost its room: the 0x80-byte record read would overwrite gPakReadBuffer");

/* the boot-time Controller Pak manager: mainproc copies it to D_80100000 and calls ControllerPakMenu there */
ASSERT(pak_menu_module_VRAM == D_80100000,
       "the Controller Pak manager is linked away from where mainproc copies it (D_80100000)");
/* its RAM must end below the two frame buffers it clears and draws into (0x80200000, 0x80225800) */
ASSERT(pak_menu_module_BSS_END <= 0x80200000,
       "the Controller Pak manager has outgrown its RAM: it would reach the frame buffers at 0x80200000");
